Receipt: lets a third party check any rating we receive against Base.\n    const receipt: Receipt = {\n      id: randomUUID(),\n      resource: RESOURCE,\n      requestHash: hashJson(parsed),\n      responseHash: hashJson(forecast),\n      payer,\n      amount: CONFIG.priceAtomic.toString(),\n      asset: CONFIG.asset,\n      network: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}`,\n      settlementTx: settlement.transaction,\n      issuedAt: nowSec(),\n    };\n    receipt.signature = await signer.signMessage({ message: hashJson(receipt) });\n    receipts.set(receipt.id, receipt);\n\n    const feedback = await issueFeedbackAuth(payer, receipt);","newString":"    // 4. Receipt: lets a third party check any rating we receive against Base.\n    const body: ReceiptBody = {\n      id: randomUUID(),\n      resource: RESOURCE,\n      requestHash: hashJson(parsed),\n      responseHash: hashJson(forecast),\n      payer,\n      amount: CONFIG.priceAtomic.toString(),\n      asset: CONFIG.asset,\n      network: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}`,\n      settlementTx: settlement.transaction,\n      issuedAt: nowSec(),\n    };\n    // Sign the body, then freeze the signed document as bytes: what we hash is\n    // byte-for-byte what /receipts/:id returns.\n    const doc = JSON.stringify({ ...body, signature: await signer.signMessage({ message: hashJson(body) }) });\n    const receipt: StoredReceipt = { id: body.id, payer, doc, hash: keccak256(toBytes(doc)) };\n    receipts.set(receipt.id, receipt);\n\n    const feedback = await issueFeedbackAuth(payer, receipt);","originalFile":"/**\n * forecast.example.com — x402-paid weather forecasts for autonomous agents.\n *\n * $0.35 USDC on Base, per call. No accounts, no API keys, no ETH required from\n * the caller. Discovery via ERC-8004 Identity Registry + A2A Agent Card;\n * ratings via ERC-8004 Reputation Registry. See design.md.\n *\n * ASSUMPTIONS to pin before mainnet:\n *  - ERC-8004 registry addresses for chainId 8453 (config below).\n *  - The FeedbackAuth EIP-712 domain + struct MUST match the deployed\n *    ReputationRegistry implementation. Verified at boot by assertFeedbackAuthAbi().\n *  - Nonce/receipt/counter stores are in-memory here: single replica only.\n */\n\nimport { createServer, type IncomingMessage, type ServerResponse } from \"node:http\";\nimport { URL } from \"node:url\";\nimport { randomUUID } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport { keccak256, toBytes, getAddress, isAddress, type Hex } from \"viem\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Config\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst env = (k: string, fallback?: string): string => {\n  const v = process.env[k] ?? fallback;\n  if (v === undefined) throw new Error(`missing env ${k}`);\n  return v;\n};\n\nconst CONFIG = {\n  port: Number(env(\"PORT\", \"8402\")),\n  baseUrl: env(\"BASE_URL\", \"https://forecast.example.com\").replace(/\\/$/, \"\"),\n\n  // Payment\n  chainId: 8453,\n  network: \"base\" as const,\n  asset: getAddress(env(\"ASSET_ADDRESS\", \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\")), // USDC\n  assetDomain: { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" }, // USDC EIP-712 domain on Base\n  payTo: getAddress(env(\"PAY_TO\", \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\")),\n  priceAtomic: 350_000n, // $0.35, USDC has 6 decimals\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: 60,\n\n  // x402 facilitator: verifies signatures and broadcasts the EIP-3009 transfer,\n  // paying the gas so the caller never needs ETH.\n  facilitatorUrl: env(\"FACILITATOR_URL\", \"https://x402.org/facilitator\").replace(/\\/$/, \"\"),\n  facilitatorApiKey: process.env.FACILITATOR_API_KEY,\n\n  // ERC-8004\n  agentId: env(\"AGENT_ID\", \"0\"),\n  identityRegistry: getAddress(env(\"IDENTITY_REGISTRY\", \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\")),\n  reputationRegistry: getAddress(env(\"REPUTATION_REGISTRY\", \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\")),\n\n  // Signs FeedbackAuth and receipts. Use a KMS in production.\n  signerKey: env(\"SIGNER_PRIVATE_KEY\", \"0x\" + \"11\".repeat(32)) as Hex,\n\n  weatherApiUrl: process.env.WEATHER_API_URL,\n  weatherApiKey: process.env.WEATHER_API_KEY,\n\n  feedbackAuthTtlSeconds: 24 * 60 * 60, // rate the forecast after reality happens\n};\n\nconst signer = privateKeyToAccount(CONFIG.signerKey);\nconst RESOURCE = `${CONFIG.baseUrl}/forecast`;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// State. In-memory => single replica. Move to Redis/Postgres before scaling out;\n// a per-replica nonce set is a replay hole behind a load balancer.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst spentNonces = new Map<string, number>(); // nonce -> expiry (unix seconds)\nconst receipts = new Map<string, StoredReceipt>();\nconst paidCalls = new Map<string, number>(); // payer (lowercase) -> settled call count\n\nsetInterval(() => {\n  const now = nowSec();\n  for (const [nonce, exp] of spentNonces) if (exp < now) spentNonces.delete(nonce);\n}, 60_000).unref();\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface PaymentRequirements {\n  scheme: \"exact\";\n  network: string;\n  maxAmountRequired: string;\n  resource: string;\n  description: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  payTo: string;\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: number;\n  asset: string;\n  outputSchema?: unknown;\n  extra?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface Authorization {\n  from: string;\n  to: string;\n  value: string;\n  validAfter: string;\n  validBefore: string;\n  nonce: string;\n}\n\ninterface PaymentPayload {\n  x402Version: number;\n  scheme: string;\n  network: string;\n  payload: { signature: string; authorization: Authorization };\n}\n\ninterface SettleResponse {\n  success: boolean;\n  errorReason?: string;\n  transaction?: string;\n  network?: string;\n  payer?: string;\n}\n\n/** The document served at /receipts/:id, minus its own signature. */\ninterface ReceiptBody {\n  id: string;\n  resource: string;\n  requestHash: Hex;\n  responseHash: Hex;\n  payer: string;\n  amount: string;\n  asset: string;\n  network: string;\n  settlementTx?: string;\n  issuedAt: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Stored as exact bytes. ERC-8004's `filehash` is the hash of the file at\n * `fileuri`, so the bytes we hash and the bytes we serve must be identical —\n * re-serializing at read time (different key order, different indentation)\n * would hand raters a hash that never verifies.\n */\ninterface StoredReceipt {\n  id: string;\n  payer: string;\n  doc: string;\n  hash: Hex;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst nowSec = () => Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);\nconst b64encode = (v: unknown) => Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(v), \"utf8\").toString(\"base64\");\nconst b64decode = <T>(s: string): T => JSON.parse(Buffer.from(s, \"base64\").toString(\"utf8\")) as T;\nconst hashJson = (v: unknown): Hex => keccak256(toBytes(JSON.stringify(v)));\n\nfunction sendJson(res: ServerResponse, status: number, body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) {\n  const payload = JSON.stringify(body, null, 2);\n  res.writeHead(status, {\n    \"content-type\": \"application/json; charset=utf-8\",\n    \"content-length\": Buffer.byteLength(payload),\n    \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",\n    \"access-control-expose-headers\": \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE\",\n    ...headers,\n  });\n  res.end(payload);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Payment requirements — what a caller must pay, advertised in every 402\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA = {\n  type: \"object\",\n  required: [\"location\", \"generatedAt\", \"periods\"],\n  properties: {\n    location: {\n      type: \"object\",\n      required: [\"lat\", \"lon\"],\n      properties: { lat: { type: \"number\" }, lon: { type: \"number\" } },\n    },\n    generatedAt: { type: \"string\", format: \"date-time\" },\n    periods: {\n      type: \"array\",\n      items: {\n        type: \"object\",\n        required: [\"startTime\", \"temperatureC\", \"precipitationProbability\", \"windSpeedKph\", \"summary\"],\n        properties: {\n          startTime: { type: \"string\", format: \"date-time\" },\n          temperatureC: { type: \"number\" },\n          precipitationProbability: { type: \"number\", minimum: 0, maximum: 1 },\n          windSpeedKph: { type: \"number\" },\n          summary: { type: \"string\" },\n        },\n      },\n    },\n  },\n} as const;\n\nfunction paymentRequirements(): PaymentRequirements {\n  return {\n    scheme: \"exact\",\n    network: CONFIG.network,\n    maxAmountRequired: CONFIG.priceAtomic.toString(),\n    resource: RESOURCE,\n    description: \"48-hour point weather forecast, hourly for the first 24h\",\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    payTo: CONFIG.payTo,\n    maxTimeoutSeconds: CONFIG.maxTimeoutSeconds,\n    asset: CONFIG.asset,\n    outputSchema: FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA,\n    // EIP-712 domain fields the client needs to build the\n    // TransferWithAuthorization digest against USDC.\n    extra: CONFIG.assetDomain,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction require402(res: ServerResponse, error: string, status = 402) {\n  sendJson(res, status, {\n    x402Version: 1,\n    error,\n    accepts: [paymentRequirements()],\n  });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Agent Card (A2A) — the offchain half of the ERC-8004 identity binding\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction agentCard() {\n  return {\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description:\n      \"Point weather forecasts for autonomous agents. 48h horizon, hourly for the first 24h. \" +\n      \"Paid per call in USDC on Base via x402; no account, key, or subscription.\",\n    url: `${CONFIG.baseUrl}/`,\n    preferredTransport: \"JSONRPC\",\n    version: \"1.0.0\",\n    provider: { organization: \"forecast.example.com\", url: CONFIG.baseUrl },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    capabilities: {\n      streaming: false,\n      pushNotifications: false,\n      extensions: [\n        {\n          // Price and asset are discoverable without making a call.\n          uri: \"https://x402.org/extensions/v1\",\n          description: \"Per-call payment over HTTP 402.\",\n          required: true,\n          params: {\n            x402Version: 1,\n            accepts: [paymentRequirements()],\n          },\n        },\n      ],\n    },\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"point-forecast\",\n        name: \"Point weather forecast\",\n        description:\n          \"Given a latitude and longitude, returns a 48-hour forecast: temperature, \" +\n          \"precipitation probability, wind speed and a text summary per period.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"x402\", \"paid\"],\n        examples: [`GET ${RESOURCE}?lat=52.52&lon=13.405`],\n        inputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n        outputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n      },\n    ],\n    // Closes the binding loop: IdentityRegistry.tokenURI(agentId) points back\n    // at this document. A client verifies both directions before trusting it.\n    registrations: [\n      {\n        agentId: CONFIG.agentId,\n        agentRegistry: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}:${CONFIG.identityRegistry}`,\n        signature: undefined, // optional JWS; set on the IPFS copy\n      },\n    ],\n    trustModels: [\"reputation\", \"crypto-economic\"],\n    // Everything a cold client needs to judge us, and where to write its own rating.\n    additionalInterfaces: [\n      { transport: \"HTTP+JSON\", url: RESOURCE },\n      { transport: \"HTTP+JSON\", url: `${CONFIG.baseUrl}/receipts/{receiptId}` },\n    ],\n    x402: {\n      payTo: CONFIG.payTo,\n      asset: CONFIG.asset,\n      network: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}`,\n      price: { amount: CONFIG.priceAtomic.toString(), decimals: 6, display: \"$0.35\" },\n    },\n    reputation: {\n      registry: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}:${CONFIG.reputationRegistry}`,\n      agentId: CONFIG.agentId,\n      feedbackAuthSigner: signer.address,\n      note:\n        \"One FeedbackAuth is issued per settled payment, so every rating on this \" +\n        \"agent corresponds to a real onchain USDC transfer to payTo.\",\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Facilitator client\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function facilitator<T>(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<T> {\n  const res = await fetch(`${CONFIG.facilitatorUrl}${path}`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: {\n      \"content-type\": \"application/json\",\n      ...(CONFIG.facilitatorApiKey ? { authorization: `Bearer ${CONFIG.facilitatorApiKey}` } : {}),\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify(body),\n    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(CONFIG.maxTimeoutSeconds * 1000),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`facilitator ${path} ${res.status}: ${await res.text()}`);\n  return (await res.json()) as T;\n}\n\nconst verifyPayment = (paymentPayload: PaymentPayload, paymentRequirements: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitator<{ isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: string }>(\"/verify\", {\n    x402Version: 1,\n    paymentPayload,\n    paymentRequirements,\n  });\n\nconst settlePayment = (paymentPayload: PaymentPayload, paymentRequirements: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitator<SettleResponse>(\"/settle\", { x402Version: 1, paymentPayload, paymentRequirements });\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Local pre-checks. Cheap, and they catch the one thing the facilitator can't:\n// a concurrent replay of the same authorization before it lands onchain.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction checkPayload(p: PaymentPayload): string | null {\n  if (p?.x402Version !== 1) return \"unsupported x402Version\";\n  if (p.scheme !== \"exact\") return `unsupported scheme ${p.scheme}`;\n  if (p.network !== CONFIG.network) return `unsupported network ${p.network}`;\n\n  const a = p.payload?.authorization;\n  if (!a || !p.payload?.signature) return \"malformed payment payload\";\n  if (!isAddress(a.from) || !isAddress(a.to)) return \"malformed authorization addresses\";\n  if (getAddress(a.to) !== CONFIG.payTo) return \"authorization pays the wrong recipient\";\n\n  let value: bigint;\n  try {\n    value = BigInt(a.value);\n  } catch {\n    return \"malformed value\";\n  }\n  if (value < CONFIG.priceAtomic) return `insufficient amount: need ${CONFIG.priceAtomic}`;\n\n  const now = nowSec();\n  if (Number(a.validAfter) > now) return \"authorization not yet valid\";\n  // Need headroom to run the request and get the transfer mined.\n  if (Number(a.validBefore) < now + 10) return \"authorization expires too soon\";\n\n  if (!/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(a.nonce)) return \"malformed nonce\";\n  if (spentNonces.has(a.nonce.toLowerCase())) return \"authorization already used\";\n\n  return null;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// FeedbackAuth — the caller's ticket to rate us onchain.\n//\n// ERC-8004's ReputationRegistry requires the rated agent to authorize the\n// rater. We issue one per settled payment: cannot be withheld from an unhappy\n// customer (it ships with the response), cannot be minted without paying.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst FEEDBACK_AUTH_TYPES = {\n  FeedbackAuth: [\n    { name: \"agentId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"clientAddress\", type: \"address\" },\n    { name: \"indexLimit\", type: \"uint64\" },\n    { name: \"expiry\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"chainId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"identityRegistry\", type: \"address\" },\n    { name: \"signerAddress\", type: \"address\" },\n  ],\n} as const;\n\nasync function issueFeedbackAuth(payer: string, receipt: StoredReceipt) {\n  const client = getAddress(payer);\n  const calls = (paidCalls.get(client.toLowerCase()) ?? 0) + 1;\n  paidCalls.set(client.toLowerCase(), calls);\n\n  const message = {\n    agentId: BigInt(CONFIG.agentId),\n    clientAddress: client,\n    indexLimit: BigInt(calls), // N paid calls buy N ratings, no more\n    expiry: BigInt(nowSec() + CONFIG.feedbackAuthTtlSeconds),\n    chainId: BigInt(CONFIG.chainId),\n    identityRegistry: CONFIG.identityRegistry,\n    signerAddress: signer.address,\n  };\n\n  const signature = await signer.signTypedData({\n    domain: {\n      name: \"ReputationRegistry\",\n      version: \"1\",\n      chainId: CONFIG.chainId,\n      verifyingContract: CONFIG.reputationRegistry,\n    },\n    types: FEEDBACK_AUTH_TYPES,\n    primaryType: \"FeedbackAuth\",\n    message,\n  });\n\n  return {\n    registry: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}:${CONFIG.reputationRegistry}`,\n    agentId: CONFIG.agentId,\n    method: \"giveFeedback(uint256,uint8,bytes32,bytes32,string,bytes32,bytes)\",\n    auth: {\n      agentId: CONFIG.agentId,\n      clientAddress: client,\n      indexLimit: calls,\n      expiry: Number(message.expiry),\n      chainId: CONFIG.chainId,\n      identityRegistry: CONFIG.identityRegistry,\n      signerAddress: signer.address,\n    },\n    signature,\n    // Attach as fileuri/filehash so the rating is checkable against Base.\n    receiptUri: `${CONFIG.baseUrl}/receipts/${receipt.id}`,\n    receiptHash: receipt.hash,\n    suggestedTags: [\"weather-forecast\", \"x402\"],\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// The product\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface ForecastRequest {\n  lat: number;\n  lon: number;\n}\n\nfunction parseForecastRequest(url: URL): ForecastRequest | string {\n  const lat = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lat\"));\n  const lon = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lon\"));\n  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90) return \"lat must be a number in [-90, 90]\";\n  if (!Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) return \"lon must be a number in [-180, 180]\";\n  return { lat, lon };\n}\n\n/** Upstream call. Throws on upstream failure so the caller is never charged. */\nasync function produceForecast(req: ForecastRequest) {\n  if (!CONFIG.weatherApiUrl) {\n    // Deterministic stand-in so the payment path is runnable without a provider.\n    const periods = Array.from({ length: 48 }, (_, h) => ({\n      startTime: new Date((nowSec() + h * 3600) * 1000).toISOString(),\n      temperatureC: Number((12 + 8 * Math.sin((h / 24) * 2 * Math.PI)).toFixed(1)),\n      precipitationProbability: Number((0.2 + 0.1 * Math.cos(h / 5)).toFixed(2)),\n      windSpeedKph: Number((14 + 4 * Math.sin(h / 7)).toFixed(1)),\n      summary: h % 24 < 12 ? \"Partly cloudy\" : \"Clear\",\n    }));\n    return { location: req, generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(), periods };\n  }\n\n  const url = new URL(CONFIG.weatherApiUrl);\n  url.searchParams.set(\"lat\", String(req.lat));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"lon\", String(req.lon));\n  const res = await fetch(url, {\n    headers: CONFIG.weatherApiKey ? { authorization: `Bearer ${CONFIG.weatherApiKey}` } : {},\n    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`weather upstream ${res.status}`);\n  return (await res.json()) as unknown;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// GET /forecast — the x402-gated resource\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function handleForecast(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse, url: URL) {\n  const parsed = parseForecastRequest(url);\n  if (typeof parsed === \"string\") return sendJson(res, 400, { error: parsed });\n\n  const header = req.headers[\"x-payment\"];\n  if (!header || typeof header !== \"string\") {\n    // Step 1 of x402: advertise the price. This response is itself the\n    // machine-readable price list — no docs to read.\n    return require402(res, \"X-PAYMENT header is required\");\n  }\n\n  let payment: PaymentPayload;\n  try {\n    payment = b64decode<PaymentPayload>(header);\n  } catch {\n    return require402(res, \"X-PAYMENT is not valid base64 JSON\");\n  }\n\n  const localError = checkPayload(payment);\n  if (localError) return require402(res, localError);\n\n  const requirements = paymentRequirements();\n  const auth = payment.payload.authorization;\n  const nonce = auth.nonce.toLowerCase();\n\n  // Claim the nonce before any await, so two concurrent replays of the same\n  // authorization can't both slip through to produceForecast().\n  spentNonces.set(nonce, Number(auth.validBefore) + 300);\n\n  try {\n    // 1. Signature, balance, and requirement match. Handles EIP-1271 for\n    //    smart-account payers as well as EOA ECDSA.\n    const verification = await verifyPayment(payment, requirements);\n    if (!verification.isValid) {\n      spentNonces.delete(nonce); // never usable => let the client retry it\n      return require402(res, verification.invalidReason ?? \"payment verification failed\");\n    }\n    const payer = getAddress(verification.payer ?? auth.from);\n\n    // 2. Do the work. If upstream is down we bail before settling, so the\n    //    caller keeps their money and their authorization.\n    let forecast: unknown;\n    try {\n      forecast = await produceForecast(parsed);\n    } catch (err) {\n      spentNonces.delete(nonce);\n      return sendJson(res, 503, {\n        error: \"forecast provider unavailable; no payment was settled\",\n        detail: (err as Error).message,\n        accepts: [requirements],\n      });\n    }\n\n    // 3. Settle: the facilitator broadcasts USDC.transferWithAuthorization and\n    //    pays the gas. This is why the caller needs no ETH.\n    let settlement: SettleResponse;\n    try {\n      settlement = await settlePayment(payment, requirements);\n    } catch (err) {\n      return require402(res, `settlement failed: ${(err as Error).message}`);\n    }\n    if (!settlement.success) {\n      return require402(res, `settlement failed: ${settlement.errorReason ?? \"unknown\"}`);\n    }\n\n    // 4. Receipt: lets a third party check any rating we receive against Base.\n    const receipt: Receipt = {\n      id: randomUUID(),\n      resource: RESOURCE,\n      requestHash: hashJson(parsed),\n      responseHash: hashJson(forecast),\n      payer,\n      amount: CONFIG.priceAtomic.toString(),\n      asset: CONFIG.asset,\n      network: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}`,\n      settlementTx: settlement.transaction,\n      issuedAt: nowSec(),\n    };\n    receipt.signature = await signer.signMessage({ message: hashJson(receipt) });\n    receipts.set(receipt.id, receipt);\n\n    const feedback = await issueFeedbackAuth(payer, receipt);\n\n    return sendJson(\n      res,\n      200,\n      {\n        forecast,\n        receipt: { id: receipt.id, uri: `${CONFIG.baseUrl}/receipts/${receipt.id}`, settlementTx: settlement.transaction },\n        feedback,\n      },\n      { \"x-payment-response\": b64encode(settlement) },\n    );\n  } catch (err) {\n    spentNonces.delete(nonce);\n    return sendJson(res, 500, { error: \"internal error\", detail: (err as Error).message });\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// POST /feedback-auth — re-issue an auth for a settled call (lost response,\n// crashed client). Requires knowing the receipt id and being its payer.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function handleFeedbackAuth(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse) {\n  const chunks: Buffer[] = [];\n  for await (const c of req) chunks.push(c as Buffer);\n\n  let body: { receiptId?: string };\n  try {\n    body = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString(\"utf8\"));\n  } catch {\n    return sendJson(res, 400, { error: \"body must be JSON\" });\n  }\n\n  const receipt = body.receiptId ? receipts.get(body.receiptId) : undefined;\n  if (!receipt) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n\n  return sendJson(res, 200, await issueFeedbackAuth(receipt.payer, receipt));\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Router\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst server = createServer(async (req, res) => {\n  const url = new URL(req.url ?? \"/\", CONFIG.baseUrl);\n  const path = url.pathname.replace(/\\/+$/, \"\") || \"/\";\n\n  try {\n    if (req.method === \"OPTIONS\") {\n      res.writeHead(204, {\n        \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",\n        \"access-control-allow-headers\": \"content-type, x-payment\",\n        \"access-control-expose-headers\": \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE\",\n      });\n      return res.end();\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"GET\" && (path === \"/.well-known/agent-card.json\" || path === \"/.well-known/agent.json\")) {\n      return sendJson(res, 200, agentCard(), { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=300\" });\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"GET\" && path === \"/forecast\") {\n      return await handleForecast(req, res, url);\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"GET\" && path.startsWith(\"/receipts/\")) {\n      const receipt = receipts.get(path.slice(\"/receipts/\".length));\n      if (!receipt) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n      return sendJson(res, 200, receipt);\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"POST\" && path === \"/feedback-auth\") {\n      return await handleFeedbackAuth(req, res);\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"GET\" && path === \"/healthz\") {\n      return sendJson(res, 200, { ok: true, agentId: CONFIG.agentId, signer: signer.address });\n    }\n\n    return sendJson(res, 404, {\n      error: \"not found\",\n      hint: `start at ${CONFIG.baseUrl}/.well-known/agent-card.json`,\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    return sendJson(res, 500, { error: \"internal error\", detail: (err as Error).message });\n  }\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Boot checks. Fail loudly rather than serving an unverifiable identity or\n// signing FeedbackAuths nobody can use.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction assertConfig() {\n  const problems: string[] = [];\n  const zero = \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\";\n  if (CONFIG.payTo === zero) problems.push(\"PAY_TO is unset — payments would go nowhere\");\n  if (CONFIG.identityRegistry === zero) problems.push(\"IDENTITY_REGISTRY is unset — the agent card cannot be verified\");\n  if (CONFIG.reputationRegistry === zero) problems.push(\"REPUTATION_REGISTRY is unset — no ratings can be authorized\");\n  if (CONFIG.agentId === \"0\") problems.push(\"AGENT_ID is unset — register in the ERC-8004 Identity Registry first\");\n  return problems;\n}\n\n/**\n * The FeedbackAuth EIP-712 domain and struct must match the deployed\n * ReputationRegistry, or every auth we sign is dead on arrival. Verify against\n * the contract at boot (eth_call its EIP-712 domain separator / a\n * `feedbackAuthTypehash` view) rather than trusting the constants above.\n * Not wired up here because it needs the pinned mainnet address + ABI.\n */\nasync function assertFeedbackAuthAbi() {\n  // TODO(before mainnet): compare keccak256 of the local type string against\n  // the registry's published typehash via RPC; refuse to boot on mismatch.\n}\n\nserver.listen(CONFIG.port, () => {\n  const problems = assertConfig();\n  for (const p of problems) console.warn(`[config] ${p}`);\n  void assertFeedbackAuthAbi();\n  console.log(`forecast.example.com listening on :${CONFIG.port}`);\n  console.log(`  card      ${CONFIG.baseUrl}/.well-known/agent-card.json`);\n  console.log(`  resource  ${RESOURCE}  ($0.35 USDC, ${CONFIG.network})`);\n  console.log(`  signer    ${signer.address}`);\n});\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":544,"oldLines":7,"newStart":544,"newLines":7,"lines":["     }"," ","     // 4. Receipt: lets a third party check any rating we receive against Base.","-    const receipt: Receipt = {","+    const body: ReceiptBody = {","       id: randomUUID(),","       resource: RESOURCE,","       requestHash: hashJson(parsed),"]},{"oldStart":556,"oldLines":7,"newStart":556,"newLines":10,"lines":["       settlementTx: settlement.transaction,","       issuedAt: nowSec(),","     };","-    receipt.signature = await signer.signMessage({ message: hashJson(receipt) });","+    // Sign the body, then freeze the signed document as bytes: what we hash is","+    // byte-for-byte what /receipts/:id returns.","+    const doc = JSON.stringify({ ...body, signature: await signer.signMessage({ message: hashJson(body) }) });","+    const receipt: StoredReceipt = { id: body.id, payer, doc, hash: keccak256(toBytes(doc)) };","     receipts.set(receipt.id, receipt);"," ","     const feedback = await issueFeedbackAuth(payer, receipt);"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73674e38-1056-4158-a24b-8a33d17ad371","uuid":"18514eb6-da04-4b86-8f97-e4a70c832b42","timestamp":"2026-07-27T11:27:26.206Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-07-27T111524Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/server.ts","oldString":"    if (req.method === \"GET\" && path.startsWith(\"/receipts/\")) {\n      const receipt = receipts.get(path.slice(\"/receipts/\".length));\n      if (!receipt) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n      return sendJson(res, 200, receipt);\n    }","newString":"    if (req.method === \"GET\" && path.startsWith(\"/receipts/\")) {\n      const receipt = receipts.get(path.slice(\"/receipts/\".length));\n      if (!receipt) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n      // Served verbatim: keccak256 of this body is the `filehash` a rater cites.\n      res.writeHead(200, {\n        \"content-type\": \"application/json; charset=utf-8\",\n        \"content-length\": Buffer.byteLength(receipt.doc),\n        \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",\n      });\n      return res.end(receipt.doc);\n    }","originalFile":"/**\n * forecast.example.com — x402-paid weather forecasts for autonomous agents.\n *\n * $0.35 USDC on Base, per call. No accounts, no API keys, no ETH required from\n * the caller. Discovery via ERC-8004 Identity Registry + A2A Agent Card;\n * ratings via ERC-8004 Reputation Registry. See design.md.\n *\n * ASSUMPTIONS to pin before mainnet:\n *  - ERC-8004 registry addresses for chainId 8453 (config below).\n *  - The FeedbackAuth EIP-712 domain + struct MUST match the deployed\n *    ReputationRegistry implementation. Verified at boot by assertFeedbackAuthAbi().\n *  - Nonce/receipt/counter stores are in-memory here: single replica only.\n */\n\nimport { createServer, type IncomingMessage, type ServerResponse } from \"node:http\";\nimport { URL } from \"node:url\";\nimport { randomUUID } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport { keccak256, toBytes, getAddress, isAddress, type Hex } from \"viem\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Config\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst env = (k: string, fallback?: string): string => {\n  const v = process.env[k] ?? fallback;\n  if (v === undefined) throw new Error(`missing env ${k}`);\n  return v;\n};\n\nconst CONFIG = {\n  port: Number(env(\"PORT\", \"8402\")),\n  baseUrl: env(\"BASE_URL\", \"https://forecast.example.com\").replace(/\\/$/, \"\"),\n\n  // Payment\n  chainId: 8453,\n  network: \"base\" as const,\n  asset: getAddress(env(\"ASSET_ADDRESS\", \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\")), // USDC\n  assetDomain: { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" }, // USDC EIP-712 domain on Base\n  payTo: getAddress(env(\"PAY_TO\", \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\")),\n  priceAtomic: 350_000n, // $0.35, USDC has 6 decimals\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: 60,\n\n  // x402 facilitator: verifies signatures and broadcasts the EIP-3009 transfer,\n  // paying the gas so the caller never needs ETH.\n  facilitatorUrl: env(\"FACILITATOR_URL\", \"https://x402.org/facilitator\").replace(/\\/$/, \"\"),\n  facilitatorApiKey: process.env.FACILITATOR_API_KEY,\n\n  // ERC-8004\n  agentId: env(\"AGENT_ID\", \"0\"),\n  identityRegistry: getAddress(env(\"IDENTITY_REGISTRY\", \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\")),\n  reputationRegistry: getAddress(env(\"REPUTATION_REGISTRY\", \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\")),\n\n  // Signs FeedbackAuth and receipts. Use a KMS in production.\n  signerKey: env(\"SIGNER_PRIVATE_KEY\", \"0x\" + \"11\".repeat(32)) as Hex,\n\n  weatherApiUrl: process.env.WEATHER_API_URL,\n  weatherApiKey: process.env.WEATHER_API_KEY,\n\n  feedbackAuthTtlSeconds: 24 * 60 * 60, // rate the forecast after reality happens\n};\n\nconst signer = privateKeyToAccount(CONFIG.signerKey);\nconst RESOURCE = `${CONFIG.baseUrl}/forecast`;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// State. In-memory => single replica. Move to Redis/Postgres before scaling out;\n// a per-replica nonce set is a replay hole behind a load balancer.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst spentNonces = new Map<string, number>(); // nonce -> expiry (unix seconds)\nconst receipts = new Map<string, StoredReceipt>();\nconst paidCalls = new Map<string, number>(); // payer (lowercase) -> settled call count\n\nsetInterval(() => {\n  const now = nowSec();\n  for (const [nonce, exp] of spentNonces) if (exp < now) spentNonces.delete(nonce);\n}, 60_000).unref();\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface PaymentRequirements {\n  scheme: \"exact\";\n  network: string;\n  maxAmountRequired: string;\n  resource: string;\n  description: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  payTo: string;\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: number;\n  asset: string;\n  outputSchema?: unknown;\n  extra?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface Authorization {\n  from: string;\n  to: string;\n  value: string;\n  validAfter: string;\n  validBefore: string;\n  nonce: string;\n}\n\ninterface PaymentPayload {\n  x402Version: number;\n  scheme: string;\n  network: string;\n  payload: { signature: string; authorization: Authorization };\n}\n\ninterface SettleResponse {\n  success: boolean;\n  errorReason?: string;\n  transaction?: string;\n  network?: string;\n  payer?: string;\n}\n\n/** The document served at /receipts/:id, minus its own signature. */\ninterface ReceiptBody {\n  id: string;\n  resource: string;\n  requestHash: Hex;\n  responseHash: Hex;\n  payer: string;\n  amount: string;\n  asset: string;\n  network: string;\n  settlementTx?: string;\n  issuedAt: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Stored as exact bytes. ERC-8004's `filehash` is the hash of the file at\n * `fileuri`, so the bytes we hash and the bytes we serve must be identical —\n * re-serializing at read time (different key order, different indentation)\n * would hand raters a hash that never verifies.\n */\ninterface StoredReceipt {\n  id: string;\n  payer: string;\n  doc: string;\n  hash: Hex;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst nowSec = () => Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);\nconst b64encode = (v: unknown) => Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(v), \"utf8\").toString(\"base64\");\nconst b64decode = <T>(s: string): T => JSON.parse(Buffer.from(s, \"base64\").toString(\"utf8\")) as T;\nconst hashJson = (v: unknown): Hex => keccak256(toBytes(JSON.stringify(v)));\n\nfunction sendJson(res: ServerResponse, status: number, body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) {\n  const payload = JSON.stringify(body, null, 2);\n  res.writeHead(status, {\n    \"content-type\": \"application/json; charset=utf-8\",\n    \"content-length\": Buffer.byteLength(payload),\n    \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",\n    \"access-control-expose-headers\": \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE\",\n    ...headers,\n  });\n  res.end(payload);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Payment requirements — what a caller must pay, advertised in every 402\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA = {\n  type: \"object\",\n  required: [\"location\", \"generatedAt\", \"periods\"],\n  properties: {\n    location: {\n      type: \"object\",\n      required: [\"lat\", \"lon\"],\n      properties: { lat: { type: \"number\" }, lon: { type: \"number\" } },\n    },\n    generatedAt: { type: \"string\", format: \"date-time\" },\n    periods: {\n      type: \"array\",\n      items: {\n        type: \"object\",\n        required: [\"startTime\", \"temperatureC\", \"precipitationProbability\", \"windSpeedKph\", \"summary\"],\n        properties: {\n          startTime: { type: \"string\", format: \"date-time\" },\n          temperatureC: { type: \"number\" },\n          precipitationProbability: { type: \"number\", minimum: 0, maximum: 1 },\n          windSpeedKph: { type: \"number\" },\n          summary: { type: \"string\" },\n        },\n      },\n    },\n  },\n} as const;\n\nfunction paymentRequirements(): PaymentRequirements {\n  return {\n    scheme: \"exact\",\n    network: CONFIG.network,\n    maxAmountRequired: CONFIG.priceAtomic.toString(),\n    resource: RESOURCE,\n    description: \"48-hour point weather forecast, hourly for the first 24h\",\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    payTo: CONFIG.payTo,\n    maxTimeoutSeconds: CONFIG.maxTimeoutSeconds,\n    asset: CONFIG.asset,\n    outputSchema: FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA,\n    // EIP-712 domain fields the client needs to build the\n    // TransferWithAuthorization digest against USDC.\n    extra: CONFIG.assetDomain,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction require402(res: ServerResponse, error: string, status = 402) {\n  sendJson(res, status, {\n    x402Version: 1,\n    error,\n    accepts: [paymentRequirements()],\n  });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Agent Card (A2A) — the offchain half of the ERC-8004 identity binding\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction agentCard() {\n  return {\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description:\n      \"Point weather forecasts for autonomous agents. 48h horizon, hourly for the first 24h. \" +\n      \"Paid per call in USDC on Base via x402; no account, key, or subscription.\",\n    url: `${CONFIG.baseUrl}/`,\n    preferredTransport: \"JSONRPC\",\n    version: \"1.0.0\",\n    provider: { organization: \"forecast.example.com\", url: CONFIG.baseUrl },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    capabilities: {\n      streaming: false,\n      pushNotifications: false,\n      extensions: [\n        {\n          // Price and asset are discoverable without making a call.\n          uri: \"https://x402.org/extensions/v1\",\n          description: \"Per-call payment over HTTP 402.\",\n          required: true,\n          params: {\n            x402Version: 1,\n            accepts: [paymentRequirements()],\n          },\n        },\n      ],\n    },\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"point-forecast\",\n        name: \"Point weather forecast\",\n        description:\n          \"Given a latitude and longitude, returns a 48-hour forecast: temperature, \" +\n          \"precipitation probability, wind speed and a text summary per period.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"x402\", \"paid\"],\n        examples: [`GET ${RESOURCE}?lat=52.52&lon=13.405`],\n        inputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n        outputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n      },\n    ],\n    // Closes the binding loop: IdentityRegistry.tokenURI(agentId) points back\n    // at this document. A client verifies both directions before trusting it.\n    registrations: [\n      {\n        agentId: CONFIG.agentId,\n        agentRegistry: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}:${CONFIG.identityRegistry}`,\n        signature: undefined, // optional JWS; set on the IPFS copy\n      },\n    ],\n    trustModels: [\"reputation\", \"crypto-economic\"],\n    // Everything a cold client needs to judge us, and where to write its own rating.\n    additionalInterfaces: [\n      { transport: \"HTTP+JSON\", url: RESOURCE },\n      { transport: \"HTTP+JSON\", url: `${CONFIG.baseUrl}/receipts/{receiptId}` },\n    ],\n    x402: {\n      payTo: CONFIG.payTo,\n      asset: CONFIG.asset,\n      network: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}`,\n      price: { amount: CONFIG.priceAtomic.toString(), decimals: 6, display: \"$0.35\" },\n    },\n    reputation: {\n      registry: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}:${CONFIG.reputationRegistry}`,\n      agentId: CONFIG.agentId,\n      feedbackAuthSigner: signer.address,\n      note:\n        \"One FeedbackAuth is issued per settled payment, so every rating on this \" +\n        \"agent corresponds to a real onchain USDC transfer to payTo.\",\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Facilitator client\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function facilitator<T>(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<T> {\n  const res = await fetch(`${CONFIG.facilitatorUrl}${path}`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: {\n      \"content-type\": \"application/json\",\n      ...(CONFIG.facilitatorApiKey ? { authorization: `Bearer ${CONFIG.facilitatorApiKey}` } : {}),\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify(body),\n    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(CONFIG.maxTimeoutSeconds * 1000),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`facilitator ${path} ${res.status}: ${await res.text()}`);\n  return (await res.json()) as T;\n}\n\nconst verifyPayment = (paymentPayload: PaymentPayload, paymentRequirements: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitator<{ isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: string }>(\"/verify\", {\n    x402Version: 1,\n    paymentPayload,\n    paymentRequirements,\n  });\n\nconst settlePayment = (paymentPayload: PaymentPayload, paymentRequirements: PaymentRequirements) =>\n  facilitator<SettleResponse>(\"/settle\", { x402Version: 1, paymentPayload, paymentRequirements });\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Local pre-checks. Cheap, and they catch the one thing the facilitator can't:\n// a concurrent replay of the same authorization before it lands onchain.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction checkPayload(p: PaymentPayload): string | null {\n  if (p?.x402Version !== 1) return \"unsupported x402Version\";\n  if (p.scheme !== \"exact\") return `unsupported scheme ${p.scheme}`;\n  if (p.network !== CONFIG.network) return `unsupported network ${p.network}`;\n\n  const a = p.payload?.authorization;\n  if (!a || !p.payload?.signature) return \"malformed payment payload\";\n  if (!isAddress(a.from) || !isAddress(a.to)) return \"malformed authorization addresses\";\n  if (getAddress(a.to) !== CONFIG.payTo) return \"authorization pays the wrong recipient\";\n\n  let value: bigint;\n  try {\n    value = BigInt(a.value);\n  } catch {\n    return \"malformed value\";\n  }\n  if (value < CONFIG.priceAtomic) return `insufficient amount: need ${CONFIG.priceAtomic}`;\n\n  const now = nowSec();\n  if (Number(a.validAfter) > now) return \"authorization not yet valid\";\n  // Need headroom to run the request and get the transfer mined.\n  if (Number(a.validBefore) < now + 10) return \"authorization expires too soon\";\n\n  if (!/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(a.nonce)) return \"malformed nonce\";\n  if (spentNonces.has(a.nonce.toLowerCase())) return \"authorization already used\";\n\n  return null;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// FeedbackAuth — the caller's ticket to rate us onchain.\n//\n// ERC-8004's ReputationRegistry requires the rated agent to authorize the\n// rater. We issue one per settled payment: cannot be withheld from an unhappy\n// customer (it ships with the response), cannot be minted without paying.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst FEEDBACK_AUTH_TYPES = {\n  FeedbackAuth: [\n    { name: \"agentId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"clientAddress\", type: \"address\" },\n    { name: \"indexLimit\", type: \"uint64\" },\n    { name: \"expiry\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"chainId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n    { name: \"identityRegistry\", type: \"address\" },\n    { name: \"signerAddress\", type: \"address\" },\n  ],\n} as const;\n\nasync function issueFeedbackAuth(payer: string, receipt: StoredReceipt) {\n  const client = getAddress(payer);\n  const calls = (paidCalls.get(client.toLowerCase()) ?? 0) + 1;\n  paidCalls.set(client.toLowerCase(), calls);\n\n  const message = {\n    agentId: BigInt(CONFIG.agentId),\n    clientAddress: client,\n    indexLimit: BigInt(calls), // N paid calls buy N ratings, no more\n    expiry: BigInt(nowSec() + CONFIG.feedbackAuthTtlSeconds),\n    chainId: BigInt(CONFIG.chainId),\n    identityRegistry: CONFIG.identityRegistry,\n    signerAddress: signer.address,\n  };\n\n  const signature = await signer.signTypedData({\n    domain: {\n      name: \"ReputationRegistry\",\n      version: \"1\",\n      chainId: CONFIG.chainId,\n      verifyingContract: CONFIG.reputationRegistry,\n    },\n    types: FEEDBACK_AUTH_TYPES,\n    primaryType: \"FeedbackAuth\",\n    message,\n  });\n\n  return {\n    registry: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}:${CONFIG.reputationRegistry}`,\n    agentId: CONFIG.agentId,\n    method: \"giveFeedback(uint256,uint8,bytes32,bytes32,string,bytes32,bytes)\",\n    auth: {\n      agentId: CONFIG.agentId,\n      clientAddress: client,\n      indexLimit: calls,\n      expiry: Number(message.expiry),\n      chainId: CONFIG.chainId,\n      identityRegistry: CONFIG.identityRegistry,\n      signerAddress: signer.address,\n    },\n    signature,\n    // Attach as fileuri/filehash so the rating is checkable against Base.\n    receiptUri: `${CONFIG.baseUrl}/receipts/${receipt.id}`,\n    receiptHash: receipt.hash,\n    suggestedTags: [\"weather-forecast\", \"x402\"],\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// The product\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface ForecastRequest {\n  lat: number;\n  lon: number;\n}\n\nfunction parseForecastRequest(url: URL): ForecastRequest | string {\n  const lat = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lat\"));\n  const lon = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lon\"));\n  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || lat < -90 || lat > 90) return \"lat must be a number in [-90, 90]\";\n  if (!Number.isFinite(lon) || lon < -180 || lon > 180) return \"lon must be a number in [-180, 180]\";\n  return { lat, lon };\n}\n\n/** Upstream call. Throws on upstream failure so the caller is never charged. */\nasync function produceForecast(req: ForecastRequest) {\n  if (!CONFIG.weatherApiUrl) {\n    // Deterministic stand-in so the payment path is runnable without a provider.\n    const periods = Array.from({ length: 48 }, (_, h) => ({\n      startTime: new Date((nowSec() + h * 3600) * 1000).toISOString(),\n      temperatureC: Number((12 + 8 * Math.sin((h / 24) * 2 * Math.PI)).toFixed(1)),\n      precipitationProbability: Number((0.2 + 0.1 * Math.cos(h / 5)).toFixed(2)),\n      windSpeedKph: Number((14 + 4 * Math.sin(h / 7)).toFixed(1)),\n      summary: h % 24 < 12 ? \"Partly cloudy\" : \"Clear\",\n    }));\n    return { location: req, generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(), periods };\n  }\n\n  const url = new URL(CONFIG.weatherApiUrl);\n  url.searchParams.set(\"lat\", String(req.lat));\n  url.searchParams.set(\"lon\", String(req.lon));\n  const res = await fetch(url, {\n    headers: CONFIG.weatherApiKey ? { authorization: `Bearer ${CONFIG.weatherApiKey}` } : {},\n    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`weather upstream ${res.status}`);\n  return (await res.json()) as unknown;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// GET /forecast — the x402-gated resource\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function handleForecast(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse, url: URL) {\n  const parsed = parseForecastRequest(url);\n  if (typeof parsed === \"string\") return sendJson(res, 400, { error: parsed });\n\n  const header = req.headers[\"x-payment\"];\n  if (!header || typeof header !== \"string\") {\n    // Step 1 of x402: advertise the price. This response is itself the\n    // machine-readable price list — no docs to read.\n    return require402(res, \"X-PAYMENT header is required\");\n  }\n\n  let payment: PaymentPayload;\n  try {\n    payment = b64decode<PaymentPayload>(header);\n  } catch {\n    return require402(res, \"X-PAYMENT is not valid base64 JSON\");\n  }\n\n  const localError = checkPayload(payment);\n  if (localError) return require402(res, localError);\n\n  const requirements = paymentRequirements();\n  const auth = payment.payload.authorization;\n  const nonce = auth.nonce.toLowerCase();\n\n  // Claim the nonce before any await, so two concurrent replays of the same\n  // authorization can't both slip through to produceForecast().\n  spentNonces.set(nonce, Number(auth.validBefore) + 300);\n\n  try {\n    // 1. Signature, balance, and requirement match. Handles EIP-1271 for\n    //    smart-account payers as well as EOA ECDSA.\n    const verification = await verifyPayment(payment, requirements);\n    if (!verification.isValid) {\n      spentNonces.delete(nonce); // never usable => let the client retry it\n      return require402(res, verification.invalidReason ?? \"payment verification failed\");\n    }\n    const payer = getAddress(verification.payer ?? auth.from);\n\n    // 2. Do the work. If upstream is down we bail before settling, so the\n    //    caller keeps their money and their authorization.\n    let forecast: unknown;\n    try {\n      forecast = await produceForecast(parsed);\n    } catch (err) {\n      spentNonces.delete(nonce);\n      return sendJson(res, 503, {\n        error: \"forecast provider unavailable; no payment was settled\",\n        detail: (err as Error).message,\n        accepts: [requirements],\n      });\n    }\n\n    // 3. Settle: the facilitator broadcasts USDC.transferWithAuthorization and\n    //    pays the gas. This is why the caller needs no ETH.\n    let settlement: SettleResponse;\n    try {\n      settlement = await settlePayment(payment, requirements);\n    } catch (err) {\n      return require402(res, `settlement failed: ${(err as Error).message}`);\n    }\n    if (!settlement.success) {\n      return require402(res, `settlement failed: ${settlement.errorReason ?? \"unknown\"}`);\n    }\n\n    // 4. Receipt: lets a third party check any rating we receive against Base.\n    const body: ReceiptBody = {\n      id: randomUUID(),\n      resource: RESOURCE,\n      requestHash: hashJson(parsed),\n      responseHash: hashJson(forecast),\n      payer,\n      amount: CONFIG.priceAtomic.toString(),\n      asset: CONFIG.asset,\n      network: `eip155:${CONFIG.chainId}`,\n      settlementTx: settlement.transaction,\n      issuedAt: nowSec(),\n    };\n    // Sign the body, then freeze the signed document as bytes: what we hash is\n    // byte-for-byte what /receipts/:id returns.\n    const doc = JSON.stringify({ ...body, signature: await signer.signMessage({ message: hashJson(body) }) });\n    const receipt: StoredReceipt = { id: body.id, payer, doc, hash: keccak256(toBytes(doc)) };\n    receipts.set(receipt.id, receipt);\n\n    const feedback = await issueFeedbackAuth(payer, receipt);\n\n    return sendJson(\n      res,\n      200,\n      {\n        forecast,\n        receipt: { id: receipt.id, uri: `${CONFIG.baseUrl}/receipts/${receipt.id}`, settlementTx: settlement.transaction },\n        feedback,\n      },\n      { \"x-payment-response\": b64encode(settlement) },\n    );\n  } catch (err) {\n    spentNonces.delete(nonce);\n    return sendJson(res, 500, { error: \"internal error\", detail: (err as Error).message });\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// POST /feedback-auth — re-issue an auth for a settled call (lost response,\n// crashed client). Requires knowing the receipt id and being its payer.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function handleFeedbackAuth(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse) {\n  const chunks: Buffer[] = [];\n  for await (const c of req) chunks.push(c as Buffer);\n\n  let body: { receiptId?: string };\n  try {\n    body = JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString(\"utf8\"));\n  } catch {\n    return sendJson(res, 400, { error: \"body must be JSON\" });\n  }\n\n  const receipt = body.receiptId ? receipts.get(body.receiptId) : undefined;\n  if (!receipt) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n\n  return sendJson(res, 200, await issueFeedbackAuth(receipt.payer, receipt));\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Router\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst server = createServer(async (req, res) => {\n  const url = new URL(req.url ?? \"/\", CONFIG.baseUrl);\n  const path = url.pathname.replace(/\\/+$/, \"\") || \"/\";\n\n  try {\n    if (req.method === \"OPTIONS\") {\n      res.writeHead(204, {\n        \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",\n        \"access-control-allow-headers\": \"content-type, x-payment\",\n        \"access-control-expose-headers\": \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE\",\n      });\n      return res.end();\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"GET\" && (path === \"/.well-known/agent-card.json\" || path === \"/.well-known/agent.json\")) {\n      return sendJson(res, 200, agentCard(), { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=300\" });\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"GET\" && path === \"/forecast\") {\n      return await handleForecast(req, res, url);\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"GET\" && path.startsWith(\"/receipts/\")) {\n      const receipt = receipts.get(path.slice(\"/receipts/\".length));\n      if (!receipt) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n      return sendJson(res, 200, receipt);\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"POST\" && path === \"/feedback-auth\") {\n      return await handleFeedbackAuth(req, res);\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"GET\" && path === \"/healthz\") {\n      return sendJson(res, 200, { ok: true, agentId: CONFIG.agentId, signer: signer.address });\n    }\n\n    return sendJson(res, 404, {\n      error: \"not found\",\n      hint: `start at ${CONFIG.baseUrl}/.well-known/agent-card.json`,\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    return sendJson(res, 500, { error: \"internal error\", detail: (err as Error).message });\n  }\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Boot checks. Fail loudly rather than serving an unverifiable identity or\n// signing FeedbackAuths nobody can use.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction assertConfig() {\n  const problems: string[] = [];\n  const zero = \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\";\n  if (CONFIG.payTo === zero) problems.push(\"PAY_TO is unset — payments would go nowhere\");\n  if (CONFIG.identityRegistry === zero) problems.push(\"IDENTITY_REGISTRY is unset — the agent card cannot be verified\");\n  if (CONFIG.reputationRegistry === zero) problems.push(\"REPUTATION_REGISTRY is unset — no ratings can be authorized\");\n  if (CONFIG.agentId === \"0\") problems.push(\"AGENT_ID is unset — register in the ERC-8004 Identity Registry first\");\n  return problems;\n}\n\n/**\n * The FeedbackAuth EIP-712 domain and struct must match the deployed\n * ReputationRegistry, or every auth we sign is dead on arrival. Verify against\n * the contract at boot (eth_call its EIP-712 domain separator / a\n * `feedbackAuthTypehash` view) rather than trusting the constants above.\n * Not wired up here because it needs the pinned mainnet address + ABI.\n */\nasync function assertFeedbackAuthAbi() {\n  // TODO(before mainnet): compare keccak256 of the local type string against\n  // the registry's published typehash via RPC; refuse to boot on mismatch.\n}\n\nserver.listen(CONFIG.port, () => {\n  const problems = assertConfig();\n  for (const p of problems) console.warn(`[config] ${p}`);\n  void assertFeedbackAuthAbi();\n  console.log(`forecast.example.com listening on :${CONFIG.port}`);\n  console.log(`  card      ${CONFIG.baseUrl}/.well-known/agent-card.json`);\n  console.log(`  resource  ${RESOURCE}  ($0.35 USDC, ${CONFIG.network})`);\n  console.log(`  signer    ${signer.address}`);\n});\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":631,"oldLines":7,"newStart":631,"newLines":13,"lines":["     if (req.method === \"GET\" && path.startsWith(\"/receipts/\")) {","       const receipt = receipts.get(path.slice(\"/receipts/\".length));","       if (!receipt) return sendJson(res, 404, { error: \"unknown receipt\" });","-      return sendJson(res, 200, receipt);","+      // Served verbatim: keccak256 of this body is the `filehash` a rater cites.","+      res.writeHead(200, {","+        \"content-type\": \"application/json; charset=utf-8\",","+        \"content-length\": Buffer.byteLength(receipt.doc),","+        \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",","+      });","+      return res.end(receipt.doc);","     }"," ","     if (req.method === \"POST\" && path === \"/feedback-auth\") {"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

The receipt is serialized once, hashed, and served as those exact\nbytes — `filehash` is the hash of the file at `fileuri`, so re-serializing at\nread time (different indentation or key order) would give raters a hash that\nnever verifies. The rater may instead point `fileuri` at their own file; that\nis their choice and we have no say in it.","originalFile":"# forecast.example.com — service design\n\nSells a weather forecast for **$0.35 USDC on Base**, per call, to autonomous\nagents. No human in the runtime loop.\n\nEverything below is assembled from four existing standards rather than anything\nbespoke:\n\n| Requirement | Mechanism |\n| --- | --- |\n| Cold discovery, no catalog we own | **ERC-8004** Identity Registry (Base) + **A2A Agent Card** at a `.well-known` URL |\n| Per-call billing, no accounts/keys | **x402** (HTTP 402 + `X-PAYMENT` header), `exact` scheme |\n| Payer holds USDC but no ETH | **EIP-3009** `transferWithAuthorization` — payer signs, facilitator broadcasts and pays gas |\n| Post-call rating other agents can read | **ERC-8004** Reputation Registry, gated by an EIP-712 `FeedbackAuth` we issue on settlement |\n| Machine-verifiable identity binding | **EIP-712** / **EIP-1271** / **ERC-721** `tokenURI` |\n\n---\n\n## 1. Flow\n\nCold client, zero prior contact, zero ETH:\n\n```\n  ┌── discovery (no human, no catalog of ours) ──────────────────────────────┐\n  │ 1. Client scans ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry on Base for agents            │\n  │    (Transfer/MetadataSet events, or any third-party indexer of it).     │\n  │ 2. tokenURI(agentId) -> https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/       │\n  │                         agent-card.json                                 │\n  │ 3. Client GETs the card over TLS. Card declares skills, price, payTo,   │\n  │    trustModels, and registrations[] pointing back to agentId.           │\n  │    Binding is bidirectional -> nobody else can claim this card.         │\n  │ 4. Client reads ReputationRegistry.getSummary(agentId) and, if it       │\n  │    wants detail, readAllFeedback + the receipt files each rating cites. │\n  │    Decides whether $0.35 is worth it. No doc-reading required.          │\n  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n\n  ┌── payment + service ────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n  │ 5. GET /forecast?lat=..&lon=..                                          │\n  │    <- 402 Payment Required  { accepts: [ PaymentRequirements ] }        │\n  │ 6. Client signs EIP-712 TransferWithAuthorization over USDC's domain    │\n  │    (350000 units, to=payTo, random nonce, validBefore=now+60).          │\n  │    Signing costs no gas and needs no ETH.                               │\n  │ 7. GET /forecast?...  with  X-PAYMENT: base64(payload)                  │\n  │ 8. Server -> facilitator POST /verify   (sig, balance, expiry, amount)  │\n  │ 9. Server produces the forecast                                         │\n  │10. Server -> facilitator POST /settle -> facilitator submits            │\n  │    USDC.transferWithAuthorization() on Base, pays the gas in ETH        │\n  │11. 200 OK  + X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE: base64({txHash,...})                   │\n  │    body = forecast + receipt URI + a signed FeedbackAuth                │\n  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n\n  ┌── rating ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n  │12. Client calls ReputationRegistry.giveFeedback(agentId, score, tags,   │\n  │    fileuri=receipt, filehash, feedbackAuth) on Base.                    │\n  │    Emits NewFeedback -> readable by every future client at step 4.      │\n  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nSteps 1–12 are all machine-executable. The only human step is the one-time\nonboarding in §6, which happens before the service is live.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Discovery — why it does not depend on a catalog we operate\n\nThe root of discovery is the **ERC-8004 Identity Registry**: a canonical,\npermissionless ERC-721 contract on Base that we neither own nor control. We\nmint one token (our `agentId`); its `tokenURI` is our Agent Card URL. Any\nclient can enumerate the registry from chain state alone, or use any of the\nindependent indexers that watch it. If we disappear, the registration is still\nthere; if we misbehave, we cannot delete anyone's opinion of us.\n\nThe **Agent Card** (A2A format, served at\n`https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json`) is the offchain half.\nIt is self-describing: skills, input/output JSON Schema, price, asset, network,\n`payTo`, supported trust models, and `registrations[]` naming the registry and\n`agentId`.\n\n**The binding must be bidirectional or it is worthless:**\n\n- onchain → offchain: `tokenURI(agentId)` = the card URL.\n- offchain → onchain: `registrations[0] = { agentId, agentRegistry: \"eip155:8453:0x<IdentityRegistry>\" }`.\n- The card is served over TLS for `forecast.example.com`, so control of the\n  domain is proven by the certificate, and control of the token is proven by\n  the fact that only the token holder can set `tokenURI`.\n\nA client checks both directions. A squatter who points their own `tokenURI` at\nour card gets a card whose `agentId` does not match their token → rejected.\n\nWe additionally publish a copy of the card to IPFS and put the CID in registry\nmetadata, so the card survives our web host going down; the IPFS copy carries a\nJWS signed by the agent's registered signer key (also committed onchain via a\nmetadata entry), so it is verifiable without TLS.\n\nOptional, non-authoritative extras: an ENS name (`forecast.eth`) with the card\nURL in a text record, and a listing in the x402 \"bazaar\"-style facilitator\ndiscovery list. Both are conveniences. Neither is required for a client to\nfind us, and neither is a catalog we operate.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Payment — x402 + EIP-3009\n\n### Why the client needs no ETH\n\nEIP-3009 (`transferWithAuthorization`) lets the token holder authorize a\ntransfer by **signature only**. Someone else submits the transaction and pays\nthe gas. Base USDC is `FiatTokenV2_2`, which implements EIP-3009 (and, via\n`SignatureChecker`, accepts **EIP-1271** signatures — so smart-account clients\nwork identically to EOAs). In our flow the facilitator is the submitter, so:\n\n- client: needs USDC, a signing key, and nothing else. Zero ETH.\n- us: never touch gas either. We receive USDC at `payTo`; we don't broadcast.\n\nThis is strictly better than an ERC-4337 + USDC-paymaster route for this use\ncase (that also works client-side and we don't care which the client uses, but\nit requires the client to run a bundler relationship; EIP-3009 requires the\nclient to do nothing but sign).\n\n### The 402 response\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"x402Version\": 1,\n  \"error\": \"X-PAYMENT header is required\",\n  \"accepts\": [{\n    \"scheme\": \"exact\",\n    \"network\": \"base\",\n    \"maxAmountRequired\": \"350000\",          // 0.35 * 10^6\n    \"resource\": \"https://forecast.example.com/forecast\",\n    \"description\": \"48h point weather forecast\",\n    \"mimeType\": \"application/json\",\n    \"payTo\": \"0x<treasury>\",\n    \"maxTimeoutSeconds\": 60,\n    \"asset\": \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\",  // USDC, Base\n    \"outputSchema\": { /* JSON Schema of the forecast */ },\n    \"extra\": { \"name\": \"USD Coin\", \"version\": \"2\" }          // USDC EIP-712 domain\n  }]\n}\n```\n\n`extra` carries the EIP-712 domain fields the client needs to build the\n`TransferWithAuthorization` digest. `maxAmountRequired` is denominated in the\nasset's base units — USDC has 6 decimals, so $0.35 = `350000`.\n\n### The `X-PAYMENT` header\n\nBase64 of:\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"x402Version\": 1,\n  \"scheme\": \"exact\",\n  \"network\": \"base\",\n  \"payload\": {\n    \"signature\": \"0x...\",\n    \"authorization\": {\n      \"from\": \"0x<client>\", \"to\": \"0x<treasury>\", \"value\": \"350000\",\n      \"validAfter\": \"0\", \"validBefore\": \"1780000060\", \"nonce\": \"0x<32 bytes>\"\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Verify → serve → settle, in that order\n\n1. **Local checks first** (free, no network): scheme, network, `to == payTo`,\n   `value >= price`, `validBefore` leaves enough headroom to settle,\n   `validAfter` already passed, and the nonce has not been seen by us before.\n   The last one matters: USDC enforces nonce uniqueness onchain, but only at\n   settlement. Without a local nonce set, an attacker could replay one\n   authorization concurrently and get N forecasts for one payment.\n2. **`POST facilitator/verify`** — checks the signature (incl. EIP-1271),\n   the payer's balance, and that the authorization matches our requirements.\n3. **Do the work.**\n4. **`POST facilitator/settle`** — facilitator broadcasts\n   `transferWithAuthorization`, waits for inclusion, returns the tx hash.\n5. **Only then** write the 200 and the `X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE` header.\n\nThe ordering is a deliberate trade. Settling before doing the work risks\ncharging for a response we then fail to produce (bad, and unfixable without a\nrefund path that needs gas and a human). Doing the work first risks eating the\ncompute cost if settlement fails after `verify` passed. We chose the latter:\nthe failure window is a few seconds wide, `verify` already proved balance and\nsignature, and one wasted upstream API call is cheaper than one wrongful\ncharge. Settlement failure returns **402**, not 500 — the caller can retry with\na fresh authorization.\n\nWe serve `503` with an unpaid `accepts` block if the upstream forecast provider\nis down, *before* touching settle.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Reputation — ratings that are worth reading\n\n`ReputationRegistry.giveFeedback()` on Base takes an EIP-712 `FeedbackAuth`\nsigned by the agent being rated. That looks backwards at first — the ratee\ngates the rater — but it is what makes the score meaningful:\n\n**We issue exactly one `FeedbackAuth` per settled payment.** So every rating\nthat exists for us is traceable to a real, onchain USDC transfer of $0.35.\nSybil-flooding our score costs $0.35 per fake rating *and* the attacker's\naddresses are visible on Base. Conversely we cannot suppress a bad review: the\nauth is already in the caller's hands the moment the call succeeds, it is\nnon-revocable by us, and `giveFeedback` is permissionless from there.\n\nMechanically, the 200 response body carries:\n\n```jsonc\n\"feedback\": {\n  \"registry\": \"eip155:8453:0x<ReputationRegistry>\",\n  \"agentId\": \"42\",\n  \"auth\": { \"agentId\": \"42\", \"clientAddress\": \"0x<payer>\", \"indexLimit\": 7,\n            \"expiry\": 1780086400, \"chainId\": 8453,\n            \"identityRegistry\": \"0x<IdentityRegistry>\", \"signerAddress\": \"0x<ours>\" },\n  \"signature\": \"0x...\",                    // EIP-712, signed by signerAddress\n  \"receiptUri\": \"https://forecast.example.com/receipts/<id>\",\n  \"receiptHash\": \"0x<keccak256 of the receipt bytes>\",\n  \"suggestedTags\": [\"weather-forecast\", \"x402\"]\n}\n```\n\n`indexLimit` is the cap on how many feedback entries this client may write for\nus. We set it to the number of calls that client has paid for, so N paid calls\nbuy N ratings and no more. `expiry` is 24h — long enough for an agent to\nevaluate the forecast against reality before scoring, short enough to bound\nreplay.\n\n`fileuri`/`filehash` let the rater attach detail. We host a **signed receipt**\nat `/receipts/<id>` containing the request parameters, response hash, settlement\ntx hash, and timestamp, so a rating can be cross-checked against Base by a\nthird party. The rater may instead point `fileuri` at their own file; that is\ntheir choice and we have no say in it.\n\n**What a cold client actually evaluates (step 4 above), all onchain:**\n\n- `getSummary(agentId)` → count + average score.\n- `readAllFeedback(agentId)` → per-rating score, tags, author, file URI.\n- For each author, whether their address actually paid our `payTo` — verifiable\n  from Base transfer logs. Ratings from addresses with no matching payment are\n  impossible if we implemented the gate correctly, and a client that finds one\n  has caught us lying.\n- Total inbound USDC at `payTo` → real usage volume, independent of reviews.\n\n**Honest limitation:** a determined attacker with capital can still buy\nreputation (self-deal $0.35 calls, rate themselves 100). This is inherent to\npermissionless reputation. Mitigation is client-side: weight by distinct\nauthors, author age, and author's own reputation. We surface the raw data\nneeded for that; we don't pretend to solve it.\n\n### Optional: Validation Registry\n\nFor a stronger claim than \"customers liked it,\" ERC-8004's Validation Registry\nsupports independent re-checking: we can call `validationRequest([validators],\nagentId, requestUri, requestHash)` on a sample of forecasts and let independent\nvalidators score them against observed weather after the fact, writing\n`validationResponse` onchain. This gives a trust signal that is not a popularity\ncontest. It is not required for launch and is not implemented in `server.ts`;\nthe hooks (deterministic receipts with hashed inputs and outputs) are in place\nso it can be added without changing the payment path.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Dependencies\n\n### Onchain (Base mainnet, `eip155:8453`)\n\n| Contract / thing | Address | We depend on it for | If it fails |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| Base L2 | chainId 8453 | settlement finality | payments stall; service returns 402, no wrongful charges |\n| USDC (`FiatTokenV2_2`) | `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` | EIP-3009 `transferWithAuthorization`, EIP-1271 sig checks, 6-decimal accounting | no payments possible |\n| ERC-8004 Identity Registry | pin canonical Base deployment | our `agentId`, `tokenURI`, metadata entries | already-discovered clients keep working; new discovery breaks |\n| ERC-8004 Reputation Registry | pin canonical Base deployment | `giveFeedback`, `getSummary` | ratings can't be written; calls still work |\n| ERC-8004 Validation Registry | pin canonical Base deployment | optional independent validation | optional signal lost |\n| (optional) ENS on L1 | — | human-ish alias, text records | cosmetic |\n\nPin the ERC-8004 addresses and ABIs to the published canonical deployment for\nchainId 8453 and treat them as immutable config; the registries are young and\nthe `FeedbackAuth` EIP-712 domain/struct **must** be taken from the deployed\nimplementation, not from memory — a mismatch means every auth we sign is\nunusable. Verify once at boot against the deployed contract (see\n`ASSUMPTIONS` in `server.ts`).\n\n### Offchain\n\n| Dependency | Role | Failure mode |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| **x402 facilitator** (`/verify`, `/settle`, `/supported`) | validates signatures, broadcasts the EIP-3009 tx, pays gas | the sharpest trust dependency — see below |\n| Base RPC endpoint | reading registry state, sanity checks | degraded; payment path still works via facilitator |\n| Upstream weather provider | the actual product | 503 before settle, caller not charged |\n| DNS + TLS for `forecast.example.com` | the offchain half of identity binding | card unreachable → IPFS copy + JWS is the fallback |\n| IPFS pinning | durable card copy | fallback only |\n| Signer key (HSM/KMS) | signs `FeedbackAuth` and receipts | ratings can't be authorized; calls still work |\n| Durable KV (Redis/Postgres) | seen-nonce set, receipts, per-client call counts | must be durable and shared across replicas — an in-memory nonce set behind a load balancer is a replay hole |\n| NTP / clock | `validBefore`, `expiry` | skew → spurious 402s |\n\n**The facilitator.** It is trusted for liveness and honest settlement\nreporting, not custody: it never holds our funds and never holds the client's\n— the EIP-3009 authorization names `payTo` as the recipient and the facilitator\ncan only broadcast it or not. Worst case it lies about `success` and we serve a\nforecast unpaid, or it censors us and we serve nothing. Mitigations: (a) verify\nthe reported tx hash against Base via our own RPC before treating revenue as\nreal; (b) keep two facilitators configured and fail over; (c) the component is\nsmall enough to self-host, which removes the dependency entirely at the cost of\nholding ETH for gas ourselves.\n\n---\n\n## 6. What we publish and register, before anyone can find us\n\nOne-time onboarding. Requires a human once; nothing in the request path does.\n\n1. **DNS + TLS** for `forecast.example.com`. The cert is load-bearing for\n   identity, not just transport.\n2. **Deploy the server**, serving:\n   - `GET /.well-known/agent-card.json` — the A2A Agent Card\n   - `GET /forecast` — the x402-gated resource\n   - `GET /receipts/:id` — signed per-call receipts\n   - `POST /feedback-auth` — re-issue an auth for an existing receipt\n   - `GET /healthz`\n3. **Fund and register a treasury address** (`payTo`) — receives USDC. Needs no\n   ETH if we never move funds onchain ourselves.\n4. **Register in the ERC-8004 Identity Registry on Base**: one `register()` tx\n   minting our `agentId` with\n   `tokenURI = https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json` and\n   metadata entries for the signer address, the IPFS CID of the card, and the\n   `payTo` address. This tx costs gas — the only ETH the operator ever needs.\n5. **Write `agentId` back into the served card** (`registrations[]`) to close\n   the binding loop. Until this is done the card is unverifiable and a careful\n   client will refuse it.\n6. **Pin the card to IPFS** with a JWS from the registered signer key; put the\n   CID in registry metadata.\n7. Optional: ENS text record; listing in an x402 facilitator discovery list.\n\nOrder matters: 4 before 5, because the card must contain an `agentId` that\ndoes not exist until the token is minted. Ship 5 in the same deploy window or\nclients see a broken binding.\n\n### Config (`server.ts` env)\n\n```\nPORT, BASE_URL, RPC_URL\nPAY_TO                 treasury address receiving USDC\nASSET_ADDRESS          USDC on Base\nFACILITATOR_URL        x402 facilitator base URL (+ FACILITATOR_API_KEY if it needs auth)\nAGENT_ID               ERC-8004 token id, from step 4\nIDENTITY_REGISTRY      ERC-8004 Identity Registry (Base)\nREPUTATION_REGISTRY    ERC-8004 Reputation Registry (Base)\nSIGNER_PRIVATE_KEY     signs FeedbackAuth + receipts (use a KMS in prod)\nWEATHER_API_URL, WEATHER_API_KEY\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Known gaps\n\n- `server.ts` keeps nonces, receipts, and per-client counts **in memory**. That\n  is a single-replica-only shortcut; it must be Redis or Postgres before a\n  second instance exists, or the replay guard is defeated by hitting a\n  different replica.\n- `SIGNER_PRIVATE_KEY` in env is fine for a testnet bring-up and wrong for\n  production; move to KMS/HSM with the same interface.\n- `indexLimit` is derived from our own record of the client's paid calls rather\n  than read from the registry. That is the semantics we want (N calls → N\n  ratings) and is robust to RPC failure, but it means a client who already\n  wrote feedback via some other auth path could find the value stale. Reading\n  the registry's current index at issue time and taking the max of the two is\n  the fix.\n- Settlement is verified by trusting the facilitator's response. Add an\n  independent `eth_getTransactionReceipt` confirmation against our own RPC\n  before counting revenue.\n- No rate limit. Payment is the rate limit for the paid path; the free\n  endpoints (card, 402 challenge) need a normal edge rate limit.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":227,"oldLines":10,"newStart":227,"newLines":13,"lines":[" replay."," "," `fileuri`/`filehash` let the rater attach detail. We host a **signed receipt**","-at `/receipts/<id>` containing the request parameters, response hash, settlement","-tx hash, and timestamp, so a rating can be cross-checked against Base by a","-third party. The rater may instead point `fileuri` at their own file; that is","-their choice and we have no say in it.","+at `/receipts/<id>` containing the request hash, response hash, settlement tx","+hash, payer and timestamp, so a rating can be cross-checked against Base by a","+third party. The receipt is serialized once, hashed, and served as those exact","+bytes — `filehash` is the hash of the file at `fileuri`, so re-serializing at","+read time (different indentation or key order) would give raters a hash that","+never verifies.
